  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance: Treat T1 and T6 as the load-bearing modules \u2014 get them right and the rest is wiring. Build T1 BEFORE T4 (confirm wiring lazy-imports it) and BEFORE T3 (tests stub it). Build T6 BEFORE T8 (status --logs uses LOG_DIR) and BEFORE T7. T2 (pyproject marker) should land before T3 to avoid an unknown-marker warning during the first test run.\n\nCritical FLAG fixes \u2014 do not skim:\n- FLAG-001 (logging): The default stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler.doRollover advances self.rolloverAt itself; we are overriding doRollover to also rename, so we MUST replicate that rolloverAt advancement. Without it, shouldRollover() will return True on every subsequent emit after midnight, causing the handler to close/reopen/prune the active log on each log call. The fix is the explicit `current_time = int(time.time()); new_rollover_at = self.computeRollover(current_time); while new_rollover_at <= current_time: new_rollover_at += self.interval; self.rolloverAt = new_rollover_at` block at the END of doRollover (after the rename and prune). Test it with the dummy LogRecord shouldRollover(rec)==0 assertion.\n- FLAG-002 (privacy filter): Earlier drafts only walked output.text. The exported session schema has multiple shapes \u2014 dict outputs with files[].content arrays, bare-string outputs, command-style inputs. Mirror the redactor's recursion exactly via _walk_strings(value, prefix) yielding (path, str) for every string leaf; otherwise PF coverage will be strictly less than _redact_value's coverage and we ship a privacy hole.\n\nBackward-compatibility traps:\n- The confirm JSON shape today does NOT have a `privacy_filter` key. Existing tests will break if you unconditionally add the key. Only emit `privacy_filter` when pf_payload is not None (i.e., when privacy_filter.enabled is True OR is_available returned False with the unavailable hint). When the config block is missing/disabled, no key is added. Verify with one of the legacy confirm tests.\n- The new confirm kwargs MUST be keyword-only (`*, policy=..., ack_privacy_findings=...`) so existing positional callers keep passing. Do not insert them into the existing positional signature region.\n- _scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays advisory. --policy strict gates ONLY new PF findings. Do not escalate the legacy regex scan into a blocking gate (it would regress current passing tests \u2014 see assumption #3 in the plan metadata).\n\nTest infrastructure notes:\n- freezegun is required for the logging tests \u2014 confirm it is in dev deps; if not, add it to [dependency-groups].dev or [project.optional-dependencies].dev as appropriate.\n- The pii contract test must use pytest.importorskip('transformers') AND @pytest.mark.pii so it is double-gated (skipped without the extra AND excluded by default addopts).\n- For LOG_DIR monkeypatching in test_status_logs_*, patch `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` (the source-of-truth attribute) and ensure _status_logs reads via `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` attribute access at call time, not via `from .logging import LOG_DIR` at module load time. Otherwise the monkeypatch will not take effect.\n\nDeferred work \u2014 do not let scope creep:\n- Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is unbuilt. Do NOT thread phase_start/phase_end into existing cli.py command paths. setup_logging and write_run_summary are a stable API; Phase 5's megaplan will adopt them.\n- Tauri Logs UI tab is explicitly deferred. Only the Python status --logs verb ships now.\n\nDebt watch (from the registry): dataclaw/cli.py is ~2420 LOC. The plan only touches confirm() (small delta), adds --policy / --ack-privacy-findings argparse args, adds the status subparser flags, and adds a small _status_logs helper. The heavy lifting stays in the new privacy_filter.py (~200 LOC) and logging.py (~180 LOC) modules. Do not move other cli.py logic in this megaplan.\n\nValidation order (in T11): unit tests cheapest first \u2192 confirm regressions \u2192 full suite \u2192 lazy-import assertion \u2192 FLAG-002 repro script \u2192 status --logs smoke. Stop and fix code (NOT tests) on any failure.",

        Debt watch items (do not make these worse):
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: inspected `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/pod.py` and `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/ssh.py`. the existing `_build_ssh_client()` helper returns the package's own `runpod_lifecycle.ssh.sshclient` wrapper, not a raw `paramiko.sshclient`. that means step 0's promise that `pod.open_ssh_client() -> paramiko.sshclient` is a 3-line public wrapper around existing logic is not technically accurate, and step 20's `scp.scpclient(ssh.get_transport())` call would not work against the current wrapper type as written. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked `runpodconfig.from_env()` in `../runpod-lifecycle/src/runpod_lifecycle/config.py`. it parses `runpod_ssh_private_key`, `runpod_ssh_private_key_path`, and related key vars, but it does not consume `runpod_ssh_password`; the current password path comes from pod ssh details inside `_build_ssh_client()`. the fallback clause therefore misstates the existing config surface when it says it reads the same env vars that `runpodconfig.from_env()` already consumes. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct: are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hugging face dependency currently installed with the repository: huggingface_hub 0.36.2 exposes entrynotfounderror, repositorynotfounderror, localentrynotfounderror, and hfhubhttperror. the plan's step 6 says a 404/not-found target shard should continue and the test stubs repositorynotfounderror, but a missing file in an existing dataset is represented by entrynotfounderror in this api family; if implementation only catches repositorynotfounderror, first-time shard paths in an existing repo can abort instead of being treated as empty. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] blocked-auto-review-command: blocked auto review command: plan tells users to run `dataclaw confirm <run_dir>` without specifying the required argparse positional path change. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] cli-growth: cli growth: plan assigns most new command behavior to an already-large dataclaw/cli.py despite its own manageability criterion. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] code-organization: dataclaw/cli.py is 1638 lines; plan adds significant new command behavior to it. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked `/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/text-ip-adapter` and its `docs/` directory: the repository still contains only an empty `docs` folder, so the plan's own must-have deliverable `docs/megaplan.md` has not been materialized in the actual repo. that leaves the explicit output requirement and the success criterion about ending with the canonical settled decisions section unmet in repository state. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements: did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the actual repository state again with `find` and `ls -la`: `/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/text-ip-adapter/docs/` is still empty, so the must-have artifact `docs/megaplan.md` still does not exist on disk. whatever happened in prior write attempts, the approved-plan requirement is still unmet in the actual repository state the review can verify. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: compared the revised fallback/auth story across both repositories. `text-ip-adapter` now plans to document `runpod_ssh_password` in its own `env.example`, but the sibling `runpod-lifecycle` config parser and readme still do not recognize that variable. if the fallback path remains in scope, the plan has not touched all supporting configuration locations needed to make that auth story coherent across the two packages. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the blocked-auto notification and review flow repeatedly reference `dataclaw confirm <run_dir>`, but the current cli only accepts confirm targets through `--file/-f` and the plan's step 11 describes `_find_export_target(hint)` without explicitly adding a positional confirm path or changing the notification to `dataclaw confirm --file <run_dir>`. if implemented literally, the command shown to users after a blocked auto run will fail argument parsing. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)
- [DEBT] does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure: does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the scheduler plan captures hf_token into launchd/systemd environment files but does not specify secure file permissions for the generated plist/unit files. the plan calls this the same trust boundary as ~/.dataclaw/config.json and mentions 0600, but the current save_config implementation in dataclaw/config.py writes json without chmod, so relying on existing config-file permission behavior is not supported by the actual repository. (flagged 1 times across 1 plans)

    "FLAG-001 regression: DailyNamedHandler.doRollover MUST advance self.rolloverAt via computeRollover() with the standard `while <= current_time: += self.interval` loop. The dedicated test test_logging_rolloverAt_advances_after_rollover is the regression guard.",
    "FLAG-002 regression: scan_session MUST recursively walk (str|dict|list) over messages[*].content, messages[*].thinking, AND tool_uses[*].input/output, mirroring dataclaw/secrets.py:_redact_value at secrets.py:229. Field paths must be dotted (e.g. messages[0].tool_uses[0].output.files[0].content). The dedicated test test_scan_session_walks_nested_tool_output_shapes is the regression guard.",
    "Lazy-import contract: dataclaw/privacy_filter.py MUST NOT import transformers/torch/accelerate at module top level. Importing dataclaw.cli must NOT pull transformers/torch into sys.modules. Verifiable via the python -c assertion in T11.",
    "Backward-compat for confirm: when privacy_filter.enabled is False/absent, the confirm JSON shape must be byte-identical to today (no `privacy_filter` key). Existing test_cli.py confirm tests are the canary \u2014 additions only, never field removals or shape changes.",
    "Confirm signature: new params (policy, ack_privacy_findings) MUST be keyword-only after the existing positional args so legacy callers continue to pass. device and min_score on every privacy_filter.scan_* function are keyword-only.",
    "Finding.fingerprint(): digest is sha256('entity|text') \u2014 score and offsets are explicitly excluded so re-runs of the same scanner converge on the same fingerprint set. Different entity labels for the same text are deliberately distinct findings.",
    "Strict block contract: when policy=='strict' and pf_payload.new is non-empty without ack, confirm MUST sys.exit(2), MUST NOT advance stage, and MUST NOT mutate known_findings. The ack path is the ONLY way new fingerprints enter known_findings.",
    "Permissive path: policy=='permissive' must NOT mutate known_findings \u2014 only logs. Acknowledgement is the gated write path.",
    "Log secret scrubbing: BOTH `hf_\u2026` literal token forms AND `HF_TOKEN=\u2026`/`HF_TOKEN: \u2026` env-var dumps must be redacted. Apply HF_TOKEN= regex first to capture the prefix, then the bare hf_\u2026 regex, so the env-var form preserves the prefix.",
    "Log file naming: today's log uses today's UTC date in the filename via DailyNamedHandler's __init__, NOT the stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler rename-on-rotate behavior. doRollover updates baseFilename to next-day's name BEFORE reopening.",
    "RUN_SUMMARY: file mode must be 0o600. Required keys validated; missing key raises ValueError. The default `warnings` value is [] when caller omits it.",
    "pyproject.toml [tool.pytest.ini_options]: BOTH `markers = ['pii: \u2026']` AND `addopts = \"-m 'not pii'\"` must be set so the contract test is excluded by default and existing test runs work without transformers installed.",
    "Scheduler/auto wiring deferred: Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is not in scope \u2014 do not invent a per-phase phase_start/phase_end wiring inside cli.py confirm/export/push paths. setup_logging and write_run_summary are exposed as a stable API for the future Phase 5 megaplan.",
    "Tauri Logs UI deferred: Phase 8's UI tab is explicitly out of scope \u2014 only the Python `status --logs` verb ships now.",
    "_scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays the default and remains advisory regardless of --policy. --policy strict gates ONLY new privacy-filter findings (which require privacy_filter.enabled=True). Do NOT escalate the legacy regex scan into a hard block.",
    "scan_jsonl and scan_shards are read-only \u2014 they must NOT mutate sessions on disk. Avoid circular imports by duplicating the small _iter_manifest_shard_paths helper inside privacy_filter.py rather than importing from cli.",
    "setup_logging idempotency: clear existing `dataclaw` logger handlers before attaching new ones, or test ordering will accumulate handlers and corrupt assertions.",
    "Module size budgets (should-have): privacy_filter.py \u2264~250 LOC; logging.py \u2264~180 LOC. Single-responsibility functions, no monolithic class.",

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance: Treat T1 and T6 as the load-bearing modules \u2014 get them right and the rest is wiring. Build T1 BEFORE T4 (confirm wiring lazy-imports it) and BEFORE T3 (tests stub it). Build T6 BEFORE T8 (status --logs uses LOG_DIR) and BEFORE T7. T2 (pyproject marker) should land before T3 to avoid an unknown-marker warning during the first test run.\n\nCritical FLAG fixes \u2014 do not skim:\n- FLAG-001 (logging): The default stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler.doRollover advances self.rolloverAt itself; we are overriding doRollover to also rename, so we MUST replicate that rolloverAt advancement. Without it, shouldRollover() will return True on every subsequent emit after midnight, causing the handler to close/reopen/prune the active log on each log call. The fix is the explicit `current_time = int(time.time()); new_rollover_at = self.computeRollover(current_time); while new_rollover_at <= current_time: new_rollover_at += self.interval; self.rolloverAt = new_rollover_at` block at the END of doRollover (after the rename and prune). Test it with the dummy LogRecord shouldRollover(rec)==0 assertion.\n- FLAG-002 (privacy filter): Earlier drafts only walked output.text. The exported session schema has multiple shapes \u2014 dict outputs with files[].content arrays, bare-string outputs, command-style inputs. Mirror the redactor's recursion exactly via _walk_strings(value, prefix) yielding (path, str) for every string leaf; otherwise PF coverage will be strictly less than _redact_value's coverage and we ship a privacy hole.\n\nBackward-compatibility traps:\n- The confirm JSON shape today does NOT have a `privacy_filter` key. Existing tests will break if you unconditionally add the key. Only emit `privacy_filter` when pf_payload is not None (i.e., when privacy_filter.enabled is True OR is_available returned False with the unavailable hint). When the config block is missing/disabled, no key is added. Verify with one of the legacy confirm tests.\n- The new confirm kwargs MUST be keyword-only (`*, policy=..., ack_privacy_findings=...`) so existing positional callers keep passing. Do not insert them into the existing positional signature region.\n- _scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays advisory. --policy strict gates ONLY new PF findings. Do not escalate the legacy regex scan into a blocking gate (it would regress current passing tests \u2014 see assumption #3 in the plan metadata).\n\nTest infrastructure notes:\n- freezegun is required for the logging tests \u2014 confirm it is in dev deps; if not, add it to [dependency-groups].dev or [project.optional-dependencies].dev as appropriate.\n- The pii contract test must use pytest.importorskip('transformers') AND @pytest.mark.pii so it is double-gated (skipped without the extra AND excluded by default addopts).\n- For LOG_DIR monkeypatching in test_status_logs_*, patch `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` (the source-of-truth attribute) and ensure _status_logs reads via `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` attribute access at call time, not via `from .logging import LOG_DIR` at module load time. Otherwise the monkeypatch will not take effect.\n\nDeferred work \u2014 do not let scope creep:\n- Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is unbuilt. The plan only touches confirm() (small delta), adds --policy / --ack-privacy-findings argparse args, adds the status subparser flags, and adds a small _status_logs helper. Do not move other cli.py logic in this megaplan.\n\nValidation order (in T11): unit tests cheapest first \u2192 confirm regressions \u2192 full suite \u2192 lazy-import assertion \u2192 FLAG-002 repro script \u2192 status --logs smoke. Extend confirm signature to add keyword-only params: `*, policy: str = 'strict', ack_privacy_findings: bool = False`. Existing positional/keyword callers must continue to pass without changes. After the existing _scan_pii call (~cli.py:1728): if config['privacy_filter'].enabled is True, lazy-import `from . import privacy_filter as pf`. If pf.is_available() is False, set pf_payload={'status':'unavailable','hint':'Install with: pip install dataclaw[pii]'} and continue (do NOT block). Otherwise call pf.scan_shards(path, manifest, device=\u2026, min_score=\u2026) when mode=='shards' else pf.scan_jsonl(path,\u2026); diff against config['known_findings']; build pf_payload={'status':'scanned','new':[\u2026],'known':[\u2026],'device':\u2026,'min_score':\u2026}. Strict block: when pf_payload.status=='scanned', pf_payload.new is non-empty, policy=='strict', and not ack_privacy_findings \u2192 DO NOT advance stage, DO NOT save last_confirm, print JSON with key `pf_new`, `process_steps`, `next_command`='dataclaw confirm --ack-privacy-findings ...', and sys.exit(2). Ack path: when ack_privacy_findings and pf_payload.new is non-empty \u2192 config['known_findings'] = pf.record_findings(pf_new, config.get('known_findings') or {}), then advance stage normally. Permissive path: policy=='permissive' \u2192 log via the (forthcoming) dataclaw.logging module if importable but DO NOT block, DO NOT mutate known_findings. Add 'privacy_filter': pf_payload to the result JSON only when pf_payload is not None (so PF-disabled output is byte-identical to today). Add a small local helper _finding_dict(f) for stable Finding serialization (include fingerprint). Argparse: add `cf.add_argument('--policy', choices=['strict','permissive'], default='strict')` and `cf.add_argument('--ack-privacy-findings', action='store_true')` to the confirm subparser, then update the dispatch at ~cli.py:2009 to pass policy=args.policy, ack_privacy_findings=args.ack_privacy_findings.",

      "description": "Create dataclaw/logging.py (target ~180 LOC) exposing exactly: setup_logging, write_run_summary, JsonFormatter, DailyNamedHandler, _scrub_secrets. Constants: LOG_DIR = Path.home()/'.dataclaw'/'logs'; _HF_TOKEN_RE = re.compile(r'hf_[A-Za-z0-9_-]{20,}'); _HF_ENV_RE = re.compile(r'(HF_TOKEN\\s*[=:]\\s*)\\S+', re.IGNORECASE). _scrub_secrets(text): apply HF_TOKEN= regex first (replacement=r'\\1[REDACTED]') then bare hf_\u2026 regex (replacement='[REDACTED]'). JsonFormatter: format() emits one-line JSON with ts (UTC ISO), level, logger, msg, optional run_id/phase/session_id/project/source/extra and optional exc; getMessage() output AND formatException output are passed through _scrub_secrets. DailyNamedHandler(TimedRotatingFileHandler): __init__ computes today's UTC date and sets filename = log_dir/f'auto-{today}.jsonl' with when='midnight', utc=True, backupCount=30. CRITICAL \u2014 override doRollover to: close current stream \u2192 recompute new_date = datetime.now(tz=timezone.utc).strftime('%Y-%m-%d') \u2192 set self.baseFilename = str(self._log_dir/f'auto-{new_date}.jsonl') \u2192 prune sorted glob('auto-*.jsonl') older than backupCount \u2192 ADVANCE self.rolloverAt: current_time=int(time.time()); new_rollover_at=self.computeRollover(current_time); while new_rollover_at <= current_time: new_rollover_at += self.interval; self.rolloverAt = new_rollover_at (FLAG-001 fix \u2014 without this, shouldRollover() re-fires on every emit after midnight) \u2192 if not self.delay: self.stream = self._open(). setup_logging(run_id, level='INFO', *, log_dir=None) \u2192 LoggerAdapter: accept optional log_dir override (production passes None \u2192 LOG_DIR; tests inject tmp_path); create dir; clear existing `dataclaw` logger handlers (idempotent); attach DailyNamedHandler + a stderr StreamHandler (level WARNING) both with JsonFormatter; return LoggerAdapter(logger, {'run_id': run_id}). write_run_summary(run_dir, summary) -> Path: validate required keys present (`run_id`, `started_at`, `finished_at`, `result` \u2208 {'pushed','noop','dry-run','error','blocked'}, `total_sessions_new`, `staging_dir`); raise ValueError on missing keys; atomic write run_dir/'RUN_SUMMARY.json'; os.chmod(path, 0o600); return path.   "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-001 regression: DailyNamedHandler.doRollover MUST advance self.rolloverAt via computeRollover() with the standard `while <= current_time: += self.interval` loop. Without this, shouldRollover() re-fires on every emit after midnight, causing repeated close/reopen/prune of the active log. The dedicated test test_logging_rolloverAt_advances_after_rollover is the regression guard.",
    "FLAG-002 regression: scan_session MUST recursively walk (str|dict|list) over messages[*].content, messages[*].thinking, AND tool_uses[*].input/output, mirroring dataclaw/secrets.py:_redact_value at secrets.py:229. Field paths must be dotted (e.g. messages[0].tool_uses[0].output.files[0].content). The dedicated test test_scan_session_walks_nested_tool_output_shapes is the regression guard.",
    "Lazy-import contract: dataclaw/privacy_filter.py MUST NOT import transformers/torch/accelerate at module top level. Importing dataclaw.cli must NOT pull transformers/torch into sys.modules. Verifiable via the python -c assertion in T11.",
    "Backward-compat for confirm: when privacy_filter.enabled is False/absent, the confirm JSON shape must be byte-identical to today (no `privacy_filter` key). Existing test_cli.py confirm tests are the canary \u2014 additions only, never field removals or shape changes.",
    "Confirm signature: new params (policy, ack_privacy_findings) MUST be keyword-only after the existing positional args so legacy callers continue to pass. device and min_score on every privacy_filter.scan_* function are keyword-only.",
    "Finding.fingerprint(): digest is sha256('entity|text') \u2014 score and offsets are explicitly excluded so re-runs of the same scanner converge on the same fingerprint set. Different entity labels for the same text are deliberately distinct findings.",
    "Strict block contract: when policy=='strict' and pf_payload.new is non-empty without ack, confirm MUST sys.exit(2), MUST NOT advance stage, and MUST NOT mutate known_findings. The ack path is the ONLY way new fingerprints enter known_findings.",
    "Permissive path: policy=='permissive' must NOT mutate known_findings \u2014 only logs. Acknowledgement is the gated write path.",
    "Log secret scrubbing: BOTH `hf_\u2026` literal token forms AND `HF_TOKEN=\u2026`/`HF_TOKEN: \u2026` env-var dumps must be redacted. Apply HF_TOKEN= regex first to capture the prefix, then the bare hf_\u2026 regex, so the env-var form preserves the prefix.",
    "Log file naming: today's log uses today's UTC date in the filename via DailyNamedHandler's __init__, NOT the stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler rename-on-rotate behavior. doRollover updates baseFilename to next-day's name BEFORE reopening.",
    "RUN_SUMMARY: file mode must be 0o600. Required keys validated; missing key raises ValueError. The default `warnings` value is [] when caller omits it.",
    "pyproject.toml [tool.pytest.ini_options]: BOTH `markers = ['pii: \u2026']` AND `addopts = \"-m 'not pii'\"` must be set so the contract test is excluded by default and existing test runs work without transformers installed.",
    "Scheduler/auto wiring deferred: Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is not in scope \u2014 do not invent a per-phase phase_start/phase_end wiring inside cli.py confirm/export/push paths. setup_logging and write_run_summary are exposed as a stable API for the future Phase 5 megaplan.",
    "Tauri Logs UI deferred: Phase 8's UI tab is explicitly out of scope \u2014 only the Python `status --logs` verb ships now.",
    "_scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays the default and remains advisory regardless of --policy. --policy strict gates ONLY new privacy-filter findings (which require privacy_filter.enabled=True). Do NOT escalate the legacy regex scan into a hard block.",
    "scan_jsonl and scan_shards are read-only \u2014 they must NOT mutate sessions on disk. Avoid circular imports by duplicating the small _iter_manifest_shard_paths helper inside privacy_filter.py rather than importing from cli.",
    "setup_logging idempotency: clear existing `dataclaw` logger handlers before attaching new ones, or test ordering will accumulate handlers and corrupt assertions.",
    "Module size budgets (should-have): privacy_filter.py \u2264~250 LOC; logging.py \u2264~180 LOC. Single-responsibility functions, no monolithic class.",
    "Do not modify tests to make them pass. If a test fails during T11, fix the underlying code. Do NOT use --no-verify or bypass hooks.",
    "CLI growth (debt watch): dataclaw/cli.py is already ~2420 LOC. Keep the new logic compact \u2014 only the confirm wiring delta and the small _status_logs helper land here; the heavy lifting stays in privacy_filter.py and logging.py."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance: Treat T1 and T6 as the load-bearing modules \u2014 get them right and the rest is wiring. T2 (pyproject marker) should land before T3 to avoid an unknown-marker warning during the first test run.\n\nCritical FLAG fixes \u2014 do not skim:\n- FLAG-001 (logging): The default stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler.doRollover advances self.rolloverAt itself; we are overriding doRollover to also rename, so we MUST replicate that rolloverAt advancement. Without it, shouldRollover() will return True on every subsequent emit after midnight, causing the handler to close/reopen/prune the active log on each log call. The fix is the explicit `current_time = int(time.time()); new_rollover_at = self.computeRollover(current_time); while new_rollover_at <= current_time: new_rollover_at += self.interval; self.rolloverAt = new_rollover_at` block at the END of doRollover (after the rename and prune). Test it with the dummy LogRecord shouldRollover(rec)==0 assertion.\n- FLAG-002 (privacy filter): Earlier drafts only walked output.text. The exported session schema has multiple shapes \u2014 dict outputs with files[].content arrays, bare-string outputs, command-style inputs. Mirror the redactor's recursion exactly via _walk_strings(value, prefix) yielding (path, str) for every string leaf; otherwise PF coverage will be strictly less than _redact_value's coverage and we ship a privacy hole.\n\nBackward-compatibility traps:\n- The confirm JSON shape today does NOT have a `privacy_filter` key. Existing tests will break if you unconditionally add the key. Only emit `privacy_filter` when pf_payload is not None (i.e., when privacy_filter.enabled is True OR is_available returned False with the unavailable hint). When the config block is missing/disabled, no key is added. Verify with one of the legacy confirm tests.\n- The new confirm kwargs MUST be keyword-only (`*, policy=..., ack_privacy_findings=...`) so existing positional callers keep passing. Do not insert them into the existing positional signature region.\n- _scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays advisory. --policy strict gates ONLY new PF findings. Do not escalate the legacy regex scan into a blocking gate (it would regress current passing tests \u2014 see assumption #3 in the plan metadata).\n\nTest infrastructure notes:\n- freezegun is required for the logging tests \u2014 confirm it is in dev deps; if not, add it to [dependency-groups].dev or [project.optional-dependencies].dev as appropriate.\n- The pii contract test must use pytest.importorskip('transformers') AND @pytest.mark.pii so it is double-gated (skipped without the extra AND excluded by default addopts).\n- For LOG_DIR monkeypatching in test_status_logs_*, patch `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` (the source-of-truth attribute) and ensure _status_logs reads via `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` attribute access at call time, not via `from .logging import LOG_DIR` at module load time. Do not move other cli.py logic in this megaplan.\n\nValidation order (in T11): unit tests cheapest first \u2192 confirm regressions \u2192 full suite \u2192 lazy-import assertion \u2192 FLAG-002 repro script \u2192 status --logs smoke.   "watch_items": [
    "FLAG-001 regression: DailyNamedHandler.doRollover MUST advance self.rolloverAt via computeRollover() with the standard `while <= current_time: += self.interval` loop. Without this, shouldRollover() re-fires on every emit after midnight, causing repeated close/reopen/prune of the active log. The dedicated test test_logging_rolloverAt_advances_after_rollover is the regression guard.",
    "FLAG-002 regression: scan_session MUST recursively walk (str|dict|list) over messages[*].content, messages[*].thinking, AND tool_uses[*].input/output, mirroring dataclaw/secrets.py:_redact_value at secrets.py:229. Field paths must be dotted (e.g. messages[0].tool_uses[0].output.files[0].content). The dedicated test test_scan_session_walks_nested_tool_output_shapes is the regression guard.",
    "Lazy-import contract: dataclaw/privacy_filter.py MUST NOT import transformers/torch/accelerate at module top level. Importing dataclaw.cli must NOT pull transformers/torch into sys.modules. Verifiable via the python -c assertion in T11.",
    "Backward-compat for confirm: when privacy_filter.enabled is False/absent, the confirm JSON shape must be byte-identical to today (no `privacy_filter` key). Existing test_cli.py confirm tests are the canary \u2014 additions only, never field removals or shape changes.",
    "Confirm signature: new params (policy, ack_privacy_findings) MUST be keyword-only after the existing positional args so legacy callers continue to pass. device and min_score on every privacy_filter.scan_* function are keyword-only.",
    "Finding.fingerprint(): digest is sha256('entity|text') \u2014 score and offsets are explicitly excluded so re-runs of the same scanner converge on the same fingerprint set. Different entity labels for the same text are deliberately distinct findings.",
    "Strict block contract: when policy=='strict' and pf_payload.new is non-empty without ack, confirm MUST sys.exit(2), MUST NOT advance stage, and MUST NOT mutate known_findings. The ack path is the ONLY way new fingerprints enter known_findings.",
    "Permissive path: policy=='permissive' must NOT mutate known_findings \u2014 only logs. Acknowledgement is the gated write path.",
    "Log secret scrubbing: BOTH `hf_\u2026` literal token forms AND `HF_TOKEN=\u2026`/`HF_TOKEN: \u2026` env-var dumps must be redacted. Apply HF_TOKEN= regex first to capture the prefix, then the bare hf_\u2026 regex, so the env-var form preserves the prefix.",
    "Log file naming: today's log uses today's UTC date in the filename via DailyNamedHandler's __init__, NOT the stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler rename-on-rotate behavior. doRollover updates baseFilename to next-day's name BEFORE reopening.",
    "RUN_SUMMARY: file mode must be 0o600. Required keys validated; missing key raises ValueError. The default `warnings` value is [] when caller omits it.",
    "pyproject.toml [tool.pytest.ini_options]: BOTH `markers = ['pii: \u2026']` AND `addopts = \"-m 'not pii'\"` must be set so the contract test is excluded by default and existing test runs work without transformers installed.",
    "Scheduler/auto wiring deferred: Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is not in scope \u2014 do not invent a per-phase phase_start/phase_end wiring inside cli.py confirm/export/push paths. setup_logging and write_run_summary are exposed as a stable API for the future Phase 5 megaplan.",
    "Tauri Logs UI deferred: Phase 8's UI tab is explicitly out of scope \u2014 only the Python `status --logs` verb ships now.",
    "_scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays the default and remains advisory regardless of --policy. --policy strict gates ONLY new privacy-filter findings (which require privacy_filter.enabled=True). Do NOT escalate the legacy regex scan into a hard block.",
    "scan_jsonl and scan_shards are read-only \u2014 they must NOT mutate sessions on disk. Avoid circular imports by duplicating the small _iter_manifest_shard_paths helper inside privacy_filter.py rather than importing from cli.",
    "setup_logging idempotency: clear existing `dataclaw` logger handlers before attaching new ones, or test ordering will accumulate handlers and corrupt assertions.",
    "Module size budgets (should-have): privacy_filter.py \u2264~250 LOC; logging.py \u2264~180 LOC. Single-responsibility functions, no monolithic class.",
    "Do not modify tests to make them pass. If a test fails during T11, fix the underlying code. Do NOT use --no-verify or bypass hooks.",
    "CLI growth (debt watch): dataclaw/cli.py is already ~2420 LOC. Keep the new logic compact \u2014 only the confirm wiring delta and the small _status_logs helper land here; the heavy lifting stays in privacy_filter.py and logging.py."
  ],

  "meta_commentary": "Execution guidance: Treat T1 and T6 as the load-bearing modules \u2014 get them right and the rest is wiring. Build T1 BEFORE T4 (confirm wiring lazy-imports it) and BEFORE T3 (tests stub it). Build T6 BEFORE T8 (status --logs uses LOG_DIR) and BEFORE T7. T2 (pyproject marker) should land before T3 to avoid an unknown-marker warning during the first test run.\n\nCritical FLAG fixes \u2014 do not skim:\n- FLAG-001 (logging): The default stdlib TimedRotatingFileHandler.doRollover advances self.rolloverAt itself; we are overriding doRollover to also rename, so we MUST replicate that rolloverAt advancement. Without it, shouldRollover() will return True on every subsequent emit after midnight, causing the handler to close/reopen/prune the active log on each log call. The fix is the explicit `current_time = int(time.time()); new_rollover_at = self.computeRollover(current_time); while new_rollover_at <= current_time: new_rollover_at += self.interval; self.rolloverAt = new_rollover_at` block at the END of doRollover (after the rename and prune). Test it with the dummy LogRecord shouldRollover(rec)==0 assertion.\n- FLAG-002 (privacy filter): Earlier drafts only walked output.text. The exported session schema has multiple shapes \u2014 dict outputs with files[].content arrays, bare-string outputs, command-style inputs. Mirror the redactor's recursion exactly via _walk_strings(value, prefix) yielding (path, str) for every string leaf; otherwise PF coverage will be strictly less than _redact_value's coverage and we ship a privacy hole.\n\nBackward-compatibility traps:\n- The confirm JSON shape today does NOT have a `privacy_filter` key. Existing tests will break if you unconditionally add the key. Only emit `privacy_filter` when pf_payload is not None (i.e., when privacy_filter.enabled is True OR is_available returned False with the unavailable hint). When the config block is missing/disabled, no key is added. Verify with one of the legacy confirm tests.\n- The new confirm kwargs MUST be keyword-only (`*, policy=..., ack_privacy_findings=...`) so existing positional callers keep passing. Do not insert them into the existing positional signature region.\n- _scan_pii (regex/entropy) stays advisory. --policy strict gates ONLY new PF findings. Do not escalate the legacy regex scan into a blocking gate (it would regress current passing tests \u2014 see assumption #3 in the plan metadata).\n\nTest infrastructure notes:\n- freezegun is required for the logging tests \u2014 confirm it is in dev deps; if not, add it to [dependency-groups].dev or [project.optional-dependencies].dev as appropriate.\n- The pii contract test must use pytest.importorskip('transformers') AND @pytest.mark.pii so it is double-gated (skipped without the extra AND excluded by default addopts).\n- For LOG_DIR monkeypatching in test_status_logs_*, patch `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` (the source-of-truth attribute) and ensure _status_logs reads via `dataclaw.logging.LOG_DIR` attribute access at call time, not via `from .logging import LOG_DIR` at module load time. Otherwise the monkeypatch will not take effect.\n\nDeferred work \u2014 do not let scope creep:\n- Phase 5 (auto handler + scheduler) is unbuilt. Do NOT thread phase_start/phase_end into existing cli.py command paths. setup_logging and write_run_summary are a stable API; Phase 5's megaplan will adopt them.\n- Tauri Logs UI tab is explicitly deferred. Only the Python status --logs verb ships now.\n\nDebt watch (from the registry): dataclaw/cli.py is ~2420 LOC. The plan only touches confirm() (small delta), adds --policy / --ack-privacy-findings argparse args, adds the status subparser flags, and adds a small _status_logs helper. The heavy lifting stays in the new privacy_filter.py (~200 LOC) and logging.py (~180 LOC) modules. Do not move other cli.py logic in this megaplan.\n\nValidation order (in T11): unit tests cheapest first \u2192 confirm regressions \u2192 full suite \u2192 lazy-import assertion \u2192 FLAG-002 repro script \u2192 status --logs smoke. Stop and fix code (NOT tests) on any failure.",
